# AI: documented functions (`ai`)

Reference table generated in the documentation round of 2026-10-31 and extended in the round of 2026-11-03. Each row is a function that reached the `documented` stage (`docs/PROGRESS.md`): its prototype, purpose, return value, side effects and callers, read from the Ghidra decompilation with the names of that round. The same text is the function's plate comment in Ghidra (`re/documented*.tsv`, applied by `tools/ghidra/ApplySignatures.java`). How the subsystem works as a whole is in [`../ai.md`](../ai.md); pointers to structures are typed `void *` and the structure is named in the summary.

Functions: 1454 (rel_duel_eng 1454).


## `rel_duel_eng`

| Address | Prototype | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0x09bf3c34` | `int ai_classify_resolve_kind(void *link)` | Classifies what a chain link's effect will do, for the AI's response decisions, into codes 0-6. By resolve handler (duel_effects_get_resolve_handler): the four destroy-target resolvers return 2 when a target is in a spell/trap or field zone (slot > 4) or a trap-monster target fails a retest with slot flag bit 12 cleared (duel_effects_call_handler_08_b), 3 when a trap-monster target passes it, else 0; the two destroy-all resolvers scan slots 0-10 of both sides with duel_effects_call_handler_08_b and return 5 (a trap monster in a monster zone hit), 4 (a spell/trap zone hit) or 0; the two negate-activation resolvers return 1 when the answered link (the previous 24-byte link, location byte at link - 0x16) is in a zone 5-10 holding a card. Otherwise a compare chain of about 60 card ids decides from the handler selector (+3 bits 4-5), the event code ((u16 +2 & 0xfff) >> 6; e.g. 0x20-0x22 give 0), the target count (+6 bits 1-7) and target slots, u16 +8 or +0xc, or spell/trap counts (0x1a11 always 6, 0x1bf1 always 4, 0x16b8 always 2, ...); card 0x1935 is classified as the card of its first target reference (instance table 0x09e2bbcc) by a recursive call. Returns the code (0 = nothing the AI must answer). Side effects: none lasting (slot flag bit 12 and link +0 are restored after the temporary changes). Callers: 47 (ai_handler_respond_to_link_1d0a_1ed5, ai_handler_draw_if_safe_1d2d, ai_handler_chain_fizzle_by_face_down, duel_core_can_send_field_card, ...); exported (2 import stubs without callers). |
| `0x09bf6728` | `int ai_is_special_summon_effect(void *link)` | 1 when the link's resolve handler is one of the six Special Summon resolvers (from Deck, from hand, revive target, Ritual Summon, revive self, from hand or Deck). Otherwise 0 unless the card has genre 3 (card_db_has_genre_mapped(card, 3)); for genre-3 cards a compare chain on the card id (link +0) decides: 1 by default, 0 for a list of ids (0x1ca7, 0x1e6e, 0x1d89, 0x1299, ...), and for the others a test of the handler selector (+3 bits 4-5 equal to 0, 1, 2 or 3), the event code ((u16 +2 & 0xfff) >> 6 equal to 2, 5, 6, 0x16 or 0x17), the location (+2 bits 1-5 >= 5 plus selector 3 or u16 +0xc == 1), or u16 +0xc (0x1a1c: == 3, 0x15f7: == 2, 0x1a1a: the value itself); 0x199e also accepts selector 2 or a non-zero upper half of the event argument (+0x14). Returns that class (0 = not a Special Summon effect). No side effects. Callers: 12 (ai_evaluate_card_option, ai_push_candidate_card, ai_push_candidate_link, ai_estimate_off_field_atk_for_card, ...); exported (2 import stubs without callers). |

The self-exclusions (-1 in location 0xb, 0xe, 0xf) suggest 0xb hand, 0xe Graveyard, 0xf banished (medium). Returns the estimate, floored at 0. No side effects. | `0x09ce82dc` | `int ai_check_target_slot(void *link, int side, int slot)` | The AI's extra target check behind ai_filter_target_mask and ai_is_valid_target: 1 when the AI treats (side, slot) as a usable target of link, 0 otherwise. It returns 0 when the slot's visible card is empty; when the recursion counter 0x09e28f80 is >= 0 (AI callers such as ai_get_card_target_mask decrement it first), link is NULL or duel_effects_cannot_be_negated(link, 0); when the slot is already one of the link's values (count byte +7 bits 1-7); when the card is the face-up card of a chain link (0x09e28cb4, count 0x09e28e34) that duel_core_chain_link_sends_card_to_graveyard; or when slot flag bit 8, 2 or 4 (u32 slot +0x10) is set. It then applies protections that depend on the targeting class (ai_adjust_link_targeting_class), the effect card's kind (card_db_get_kind; kind 8 counted as 0xd for some links) and genres 10/11: race and attribute protections from active 0x14ca, 0x1a07, 0x1d01, 0x1e76, 0x1dbb, 0x1ea3, 0x1f12; face-down cases (flag bit 10) of 0x18bd/0x129c and of the link cards 0x16b8/0x1086/0xffa/0x12eb/0x117b; about 30 per-card protections of the target (0x1e92 and six others against links of 0xe9e, 0x175e, 0x1c10, 0x16fe counters x 400 against the LP, 0x1d9d, 0x1ed9 Level < 7, ...); and link-card rules (0x1f22 needs duel_core_can_send_slot_to_hand, 0x1957 rejects battle stoppers, 0x13eb rejects slots holding its marker for the link's side, 0x1893 rejects slots with a direct-attack mode). No side effects. Callers: 2 (ai_filter_target_mask, ai_is_valid_target). |

| `0x09ce9edc` | `void ai_estimate_slot_stats(int side, int slot, uint *out, void *record)` | Fills the 13-word stat block out (stat-modifiers.md) for side's slot as the AI may see it. Empty slot: ids 0. Face-down card (u16 slot +0x08 == 0) without slot flag bit 10: the masked id (ai_mask_hidden_card_id) in out[0], ai_estimate_card_atk / ai_estimate_card_def(side, -1, id, 1) in out[6]/out[7], printed ATK/DEF in out[8]/out[9]; out[0]/out[1] are then zeroed unless the slot has effect 0x1362 and a monster slot holds a kind-5 marker of 0x1362, in which case attribute, attribute mask and race are filled too. Otherwise the card is temporarily marked face-up and duel_core_get_card_stats fills out. When record (an ai_stat_snapshot record, 0x44 bytes) is non-NULL, ATK, DEF, original ATK and DEF (from the estimate, or from duel_core_compute_card_stats(side, slot, out, 2, 0)) are copied to record +0x34..+0x40. No return value. Side effects: writes out and record. Callers: 18 (ai_get_best_monster_stats, ai_find_best_monster_slot, ai_sum_attacker_atk, ai_get_slot_battle_stats_as_ai, ...). |
